3327	This method is called when a record hook is triggered based on the type and ID of the record. It checks if the ID is null, if there are no hooks, or if the cluster ID is 0. If any of these conditions are true, it returns RECORD_NOT_CHANGED. It then determines the scope of the hook and iterates through the hooks for that scope. For each hook, it checks the run mode and the distributed execution mode. If the run mode is DEFAULT and the storage is distributed and the hook's distributed execution mode is TARGET_NODE, it skips the hook. If the run mode is RUNNING_DISTRIBUTED and the hook's distributed execution mode is SOURCE_NODE, it also skips the hook. It then calls the onTrigger method of the hook and checks the result. If the result is RECORD_CHANGED, it sets recordChanged to true. If the result is SKIP_IO, it returns SKIP_IO. If the result is SKIP, it returns SKIP. If the result is RECORD_REPLACED, it returns RECORD_REPLACED. Finally, it pops the hook from the stack.

```java
private void showTimeSeriesAll(java.util.List<LogReader.Log> logs) {
    TimeSeries bytesSentData = new TimeSeries("Bytes Sent", Minute.class);
    TimeSeries timeTookData = new TimeSeries("Average Latency", Minute.class);
    TimeSeries nreqData = new TimeSeries("Number of Requests", Minute.class);
    String intervalS = "5 minute"; // interval.getText().trim(); // if (intervalS.length() == 0) intervalS = "5 minute";
    long period = 1000 * 60 * 5;
    try {
        TimeDuration tu = new TimeDuration(intervalS);
        period = (long) (1000 * tu.getValueInSeconds());
    } catch (Exception e) {
        System.out.printf("Illegal Time interval=%s %n", intervalS);
    }
    long current = 0;
    long bytes = 0;
    long timeTook = 0;
    long total_count = 0;
    long count = 0;
    for (LogReader.Log log : logs) {
        long msecs = log.date;
        if (msecs - current > period) {
            if (current > 0) {
                total_count += count;
                addPoint(bytesSentData, timeTookData, nreqData, new Date(current), bytes, count, timeTook);
            }
            bytes = 0;
            count = 0;
            timeTook = 0;
            current = msecs;
        }
        bytes += log.getBytes();
        timeTook += log.getMsecs();
        count++;
    }
    if (count > 0) {
        addPoint(bytesSentData, timeTookData, nreqData, new Date(current), bytes, count, timeTook);
    }
    total_count += count;
    System.out.printf("showTimeSeriesAll: total_count = %d logs = %d%n", total_count, logs.size());
    MultipleAxisChart mc = new MultipleAxisChart("Access Logs", intervalS + " average", "Mbytes Sent", bytesSentData);
    mc.addSeries("Number of Requests", nreqData);
    mc.addSeries("Average Latency (secs)", timeTookData);
    mc.finish(new java.awt.Dimension(1000, 1000));
    // MultipleAxisChart mc = new MultipleAxisChart("

9935	If the given worker is terminating, returns false. Otherwise, if the worker is not in the queue, returns false. If the worker is in the queue, it spins for a random amount of time, then checks if the worker is still in the queue. If the worker is still in the queue, it checks if the pool is terminating or if the worker is the last waiter. If the pool is terminating or the worker is the last waiter, it returns false. If the worker is not the last waiter, it parks the worker for a random amount of time, then checks if the worker is still in the queue. If the worker is still in the queue, it returns false. If the worker is not in the queue, it returns true.
